# Review — V1 conformance audit, whole codebase (`002ffef`)
_Full V1 scope · 2,182 tracked files · 1,157 requirement rows · 178 design boards · reviewed 2026-10-20_

**Contents:** [Summary](#summary) · [Method](#method) · [Requirement table](#full-requirement-table) · [Design conformance](#design-conformance-per-board) · [Live run](#live-run-results-per-role) · [Gaps](#gaps-ranked) ([Critical](#critical) · [High](#high) · [Warning](#warning) · [Info](#info)) · [Deferrals and rulings](#items-covered-by-approved-deferrals-or-rulings) · [Not verified](#what-was-not-verified-and-why) · [Test run results](#test-run-results)

## Summary
This audit checked every V1 promise independently against the code and a live run of all three clients. The promises came from the SRS, every use case including alternates and exceptions, every milestone acceptance criterion, and every owner ruling and deferral in the milestone files and API contract headers. The tracker, the milestone reviews and the contract headers were treated as claims, not proof.

Most of V1 is really there. Of the 1,157 requirement rows:
- **implemented:** 961;
- **covered by an approved deferral or ruling:** 101;
- **partial:** 82;
- **deviates:** 7;
- **missing:** 6.

The full ride loop works end to end in the real apps, from offer and accept through arrive, start, metered completion, cash collection, an itemized receipt and a wallet tip. The admin dashboard renders every page without an error state.

The live run did, however, reproduce two **Critical** defects:
- **C1 — the account-suspension gate has a hole.** A suspended account is refused on `/rides` (403) but can still book scheduled rides and create, join and start shared rides (201). Those rides later dispatch as real live rides.
- **C2 — a wallet ride whose promo covers the whole fare can never be completed.** `complete` returns 500 on a database CHECK violation every time. The ride stays `in_progress` forever, cancel is refused in that state, and the driver and rider are both stuck.

Nine **High** findings follow. Every one was reproduced live except the route line, which was observed on screen:
- **H1:** account deletion has no active-ride or debt gate.
- **H2:** new admins have no way to set a password in the dashboard.
- **H3:** phone numbers are not canonicalized, so one handset can hold several accounts.
- **H4:** a config edit made mid-ride changes that ride's fare.
- **H5:** the incentives screen and five-star challenges let a driver attribute individual ratings.
- **H6:** the driver earnings page returns 500 once two members of a shared ride have tipped.
- **H7:** a floor-blocked driver can still claim scheduled rides.
- **H8:** admin user search finds no one by name, and finds a phone only if that user has had a ride.
- **H9:** the rider's tracking map draws no route line.

Design conformance is good. Of 178 boards, 80 match, 90 have minor differences and 8 mismatch; every mismatch has a finding below.

**Verdict:** Fail
**Findings:** Critical 2 · High 9 · Warning 43 · Info 107 · Praise 6
_Critical, High and Warning are merged by root cause: 205 scan claims went through verification, and 3 Critical claims, 20 High claims and 64 Warning claims collapse into the findings above. Info items are listed one per verified claim._

## Method
1. **Requirement list.** Four extractor agents built a numbered checklist from four sources:
   - `docs/SRS.md`: every V1 ID, split into separately checkable sub-rows, plus `PROJECT-STRUCTURE.md` structure rules → **S-** rows (342);
   - every use case, with each alternate and exception as its own row → **U-** rows (201);
   - every milestone ticket AC and milestone-level criterion, M1–M15 → **M-** rows (340);
   - every owner ruling, approved reading, amendment and deferral in the milestone files, the tracker and the 15 `backend/docs/api/*.yaml` headers → **R-** rows (274).

   Each row was assigned to one of 13 domain slices.
2. **Code scan.** Sixteen read-only agents worked over the codebase:
   - 13 domain slices, each tracing every row end to end (migration → repository → service → controller/contract → app API → screen, including permission gating and EN + AR copy);
   - 3 app-wide agents, one per client, checking role gating, i18n parity, RTL, digits, empty/loading/error states and design-language rules.

   Every status carries `file:line` evidence.
3. **Design conformance.** Five agents compared every board in `docs/design/canvas/` (178) with its implementing screen, and listed boards without screens and screens without boards.
4. **Live run** (orchestrator). The live-run section below covers the setup, the flows exercised and the evidence. Screenshots are in [`v1-conformance-2026-10-20/`](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/).
5. **Verification wave.** Seven fresh agents re-checked all 205 partial/missing/deviates/design claims against the code, the rulings and the live-run evidence:
   - 167 confirmed;
   - 20 downgraded;
   - 15 covered by a ruling or deferral;
   - 3 dropped as false positives.

   Only verified findings appear below.

In total 32 agents ran: 4 checklist extractors, 16 code scanners, 5 design agents and 7 verifiers. Nothing was fixed. Fixes go through `/labby-fix`.

**Status vocabulary in the table:**
- **implemented:** the code meets the row;
- **partial:** some of it is missing;
- **deviates:** behaviour differs from the requirement;
- **missing:** not built;
- **deferred:** an owner ruling or approved deferral covers it.

`→ C1/H3/W12/I40` links a row to its finding.

## Design conformance per board
All 178 boards:

| Verdict | Count |
|---|---|
| matches | 80 |
| minor-diffs | 90 |
| mismatch | 8 |

Board links open the static mock. "Minor differences" means copy, spacing or a state that departs from the board without changing the screen's purpose or primary action; the larger ones are findings.

#### rider-a (40 boards)

| Board | Screen | Verdict | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Main](../design/canvas/Main.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/home-screen.tsx` | minor-diffs → I36 | Now pill superseded by Later (RiderM12Home). Recent-destination row missing. Extra region chip (home-screen.tsx:358-365) and API-version line (home-screen.tsx:384) not on any home board. |
| [RiderWhereTo](../design/canvas/RiderWhereTo.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/route-planner.tsx, apps/rider/src/screens/ride/booking-screen…` | minor-diffs → I48 | Fields, Add stop, saved chips, Choose on map, matching line and See prices all match. The route rail beside the fields is missing. Search only matches saved places (no geocoding). |
| [RiderWhereToAr](../design/canvas/RiderWhereToAr.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/route-planner.tsx` | matches | Arabic copy matches. The board shows Eastern Arabic digits; the code renders Western digits per the owner ruling, so the code is correct. |
| [RiderChooseRide](../design/canvas/RiderChooseRide.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/booking-screen.tsx` | minor-diffs → I40 | Option meta shows km/min instead of seats + driver ETA/drop-off. Prices carry an 'est.' suffix. Payment row uses a Change toggle. |
| [RiderPlanComfort](../design/canvas/RiderPlanComfort.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/booking-screen.tsx (MatchingLine)` | matches | Matching mode plus the comfort summary and Change link. |
| [RiderWalletShort](../design/canvas/RiderWalletShort.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/booking-screen.tsx` | minor-diffs → I41 | Primary button says 'Choose Economy'; the board says 'Book Economy'. The wallet preview and distant-fee disclosure match. |
| [RiderSurgeChoose](../design/canvas/RiderSurgeChoose.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/booking-screen.tsx` | matches | Surge banner, multiplier per option, locked-rate note and 'Choose X · 1.5×' all match. |
| [RiderSurgeChooseAr](../design/canvas/RiderSurgeChooseAr.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/booking-screen.tsx` | matches | Arabic copy matches. The multiplier is LTR-isolated. |
| [RiderSurgeChanged](../design/canvas/RiderSurgeChanged.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/surge-changed-sheet.tsx` | matches | Chip, title, body, struck-through 'before', 'now', lock note, Confirm and Back all match. |
| [RiderFinding](../design/canvas/RiderFinding.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-view.tsx` | matches | Finding sheet matches, including 'Free to cancel now' and 'Cancel request'. |
| [RiderNoDriver](../design/canvas/RiderNoDriver.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-view.tsx` | minor-diffs → I42 | Matches, except the trip line hard-codes 'A → B' (ride-view.tsx:530). In Arabic the arrow is not mirrored, so it points at the pickup. |
| [RiderDriverOnWay](../design/canvas/RiderDriverOnWay.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-view.tsx, apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-parts.tsx` | minor-diffs → I43 | The approach progress bar is missing. The action row and Safety pill are superseded by RiderSafetyActions and RiderOnWayCancel. The rating badge and plate match. |
| [RiderOnWayCancel](../design/canvas/RiderOnWayCancel.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-view.tsx` | matches | Filled 48 px 'Cancel ride' pill in the footer. |

| [RiderWaitCharge](../design/canvas/RiderWaitCharge.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-view.tsx` | minor-diffs → I37 | The amber wait-charge card matches. Missing: the board's 'Samer has arrived' header, the 'Waiting at <pickup>' row and the 'I'm coming' button. |
| [RiderInRide](../design/canvas/RiderInRide.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-view.tsx` | matches | Headline, details line, stops, Change destination, Add stop, metered footer and connection-lost banner all match. |
| [RiderSafetyActions](../design/canvas/RiderSafetyActions.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/safety-actions.tsx` | matches | Chat/Call/Share tiles (64 px) and the red SOS pill (owner-approved #D92D20), mirrored in RTL. Adds a 'Report <driver>' link from M11. |
| [RiderSosConfirm](../design/canvas/RiderSosConfirm.dc.html) | `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/safety-actions.tsx` | matches | Copy matches in EN and AR. Cancel is a filled secondary button; the board's is outlined (cosmetic). |

## Live-run results per role

### Setup
- **Backend:** jar built from `002ffef` (`-DskipTests`), run with the dev profile against the local `labby-postgres` on port 5433. That database holds 3,057 users, including the 3,050 "Load …" accounts, which were left untouched. Production was never touched.
- **SMS:** `SMS_GATEWAY_URL` was first pointed at a dead address (`127.0.0.1:9`). With it, every OTP request returned 502 and no code was stored, so register, reset, 2FA and delete could not be exercised at all. The URL was then switched to a catch-all receiver on `127.0.0.1:9099`, so nothing left the machine and the codes were read from its log.
- **Push:** Firebase credentials were left empty, so `LoggingPushSender` was used and no push left the machine.
- **Ride simulator:** on for the first start only, then off so rides could be driven deliberately.
- **Mobile:** emulator `labby` with adb, driver Metro on 8081, rider Metro on 8083, deep links `labbydriver://` and `labbyrider://`, and `adb emu geo fix` feeds.
  - Emulator limitation: the rider app asks for `Balanced` accuracy, which the emulator can only answer once another app has requested GPS. It worked after the driver app was opened. This is not an app defect.
- **Admin:** Vite dev server on 5173. Screens were driven through headless Firefox (geckodriver) so screenshots could be saved, and also checked in the browser pane.
- **API probes:** a small Python harness against the running backend, with the seeded dev accounts from `backend/docs/SEEDING.md` (`+963900000001…7`).
- **Data changed in the local DB:**
  - wallet grants to riders 001 and 003, and a promo `AUDITZERO`;
  - settlements for drivers 005, 006 and 007;
  - rider 003 suspended and reactivated;
  - riders 003 and driver 005 deleted and then reactivated by re-registering;
  - the Comfort rates edited and restored, and Economy disabled and re-enabled;
  - availability windows for driver 005, and two ratings by rider 003;
  - ride `b7b60b90`, permanently stuck by C2, moved to `cancelled_by_driver` by SQL so the accounts could be used again.

### Rider
| Flow | Result | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Register with OTP / reactivation (UC-S-01/08) | OTP issued, and the code was delivered to the local receiver. Re-registering a deleted phone returned `mode: reactivated`, and login then worked with the old password. Three spellings of one number each got their own register OTP (**H3**). A completely new account was not taken through `complete`. | API probe 4 |
| Home, where-to, drop a pin, choose a ride (UC-R-04) | Three service types, each with an estimate labelled as an estimate, plus promo and payment rows. Booking dispatched straight away; with no candidate the rider got "No driver available · You were not charged" with Try again (RIDE-08). | [home](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-home-en.png) · [where-to](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-where-to-en.png) · [pin](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-drop-pin-en.png) · [choose](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-choose-ride-en.png) · [no driver](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-no-driver-en.png) |
| Ride with a real driver, end to end | Booked → driver 005 accepted in the driver app → driver on the way → in ride → completed → cash confirmed → itemized receipt from the stored fare. No vehicle photo (**W32**). No route line on the rider map while the driver map has one (**H9**). "Report {name}" clipped in Arabic (**W2**). | [on the way](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-driver-on-way-ar.png) · [in ride](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-in-ride-ar.png) · [receipt](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-after-complete-ar.png) |
| Tip (PAY-04) | Suggested amounts and a custom field, paid "from your wallet". 2,000 SYP sent, landing as a `tip` ledger entry, commission-free; the wallet shows the debit. | [tip](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-tip-ar.png) · [sent](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-tip-sent-ar.png) · [wallet](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-en-wallet.png) |
| Rating (RATE-01) | Prompted after the ride, skippable, submitted. In Arabic the title "…مع عمر؟" loses the name (**W2**); in English it renders. | [AR rating](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-rate-shared-ar-no-name.png) |
| Cancel | The rider cancel API was exercised for scheduled and shared rides (200). Cancelling through the app after acceptance was not clicked. | API probe 1 |
| Scheduled rides (RIDE-S) | Booking works through the API (201). The schedule picker and the empty list render. **A suspended account can also schedule (C1).** | [schedule](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-en-schedule.png) · [scheduled](v1-conformance-2026-10-20/rider-en-scheduled.png) |

### Live-run findings not raised by the static scan
- **W1:** the admin shows a raw i18n key for shared-ride payment status.
- **W2:** the rider app clips names inside short Arabic strings.
- **W3:** the balance floor and warning margin are not currency-scaled, and there is no admin field for the floor.
- **I106:** an unsupported HTTP method returns 500.
- **I107:** Arabic names inside LTR admin lines are not bidi-isolated.

The live run also turned the static zero-total suspicion into a confirmed permanent stuck state (C2), and promoted H1, H3–H8 from code reading to observed behaviour.

## Gaps ranked

## Critical

### C1 — Suspended accounts can book scheduled rides and create, join and start shared rides
- **Category:** security · **Severity:** Critical · **Certainty:** High (reproduced)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/security/AccountSuspensionInterceptor.java:51-61`; `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/RideAccess.java:43-55`; `ScheduledRideServiceImpl.java:61-62`; `SharedRideServiceImpl.java:105,260,384`
- **Claims:** `scheduled-shared:S-097` (USR-06 G1), `scheduled-shared:S-111` (G2)
- **Rationale:**
  - The suspension gate covers only the paths in `FEATURE_PATHS` (rides, rider, driver, wallet, chat, routing, ratings, reports, report-files).
  - `/api/v1/scheduled-rides` and `/api/v1/shared-rides` are not on that list.
  - Their services call only `rideAccess.requireActiveRider`, which checks the rider role state, never `users.state`.
  - Live: rider 003 with a whole-account suspension got `403 account_suspended` on `POST /rides`, but `201` on `POST /scheduled-rides` and `201` on `POST /shared-rides`.
  - At T − lead those rides convert, or fall back, into real dispatched rides, so USR-06 suspension is bypassed.
  - No ruling exempts these paths; the only exemptions are SOS and finishing a ride.
- **Fix Suggestion:** add `/api/v1/scheduled-rides/**` and `/api/v1/shared-rides/**` to `FEATURE_PATHS`, or make `requireActiveRider` also refuse `users.state != active` with `account_suspended`. Extend the `TrustAdminIntegrationTest` refused-path list, which passed 8/8 in this audit without covering these paths.

### C2 — A wallet ride whose promo covers the whole fare can never be completed
- **Category:** money / correctness · **Severity:** Critical · **Certainty:** High (reproduced)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/WalletServiceImpl.java:99-108` (`debitUpTo`); `V12__money.sql:54-58` (`ck_wallet_transactions_amount`); called from `RideMoneyServiceImpl.java:92` (and `:118` for shared members)
- **Claim:** `money:U-021` (G1)
- **Rationale:**
  - `FareCalculator.applyDiscount` clamps the total to 0.
  - `debitUpTo` returns early only when the balance is ≤ 0. Otherwise it debits `balance.min(0.00)` and inserts a `0.00` `fare_payment` row, which the CHECK constraint rejects.
  - Live: a wallet ride with a fixed promo larger than the fare and a 5,000 SYP balance got `POST /driver/rides/{id}/complete` → **500**, every retry.
  - Driver cancel is then refused with `409 illegal_state in_progress`.
  - The ride is stuck forever and blocks the driver (one live ride) and the rider (one active ride).
  - Any rider holding wallet credit who uses a promo at least as large as a short fare hits this. The shared-member path has the same flaw.
- **Fix Suggestion:** in `debitUpTo` (and `collectUpTo`), return `0` without writing when `maxAmount ≤ 0` or the debit is 0. Add an integration test: wallet ride, positive balance, fixed promo ≥ fare, then `complete` → 200 and `paid_wallet`. Repair any stuck rides with a one-off script. The related cash case, where the driver is asked to collect SYP 0, is W29.

## High

### H1 — Account deletion has no active-ride or debt gate (UC-S-07 E1/E2)
- **Category:** srs-conformance · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** High (reproduced)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/MeServiceImpl.java:161-175` (comment at `:165-166`: "No precondition checks in M2 — active-ride / driver-debt gates arrive with their milestones")
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:U-166`, `auth-accounts:U-167`, `auth-accounts:M-047`, `auth-accounts:M-034` (missing exception-path tests)
- **Rationale:**
  - `auth-v1.yaml:243` reserves a 409 for "deletion blocked by an active precondition", and UC-S-07 lists "no active ride" (E1) and "debt below floor pending settlement" (E2).
  - The gates were deferred to later milestones and never added.
  - Live: rider 003 deleted the account with a ride `in_progress` (204), and the driver then completed a ride for a deleted account.
  - Live: driver 005, blocked at −5,625 SYP, deleted the account (204), so a driver can walk away from cash-commission debt.
  - The related cleanup gap (pending requests and the online flag survive deletion) is W13.
- **Fix Suggestion:** before the OTP is consumed, throw `409 active_ride` when the user is rider or driver on an assigned or pre-acceptance ride, and `409 debt_below_floor` when a driver balance is at or below the region floor. Map both in the apps (they already map 409 to deletion-blocked copy). Add integration tests for both paths.

### H2 — A newly created admin cannot set a password in the dashboard (UC-A-01)
- **Category:** srs-conformance · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** High (observed)
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/pages/LoginPage.tsx`; `apps/admin/src/api/auth.ts:48-70`; backend `AdminAccountServiceImpl.java:186-199`
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:U-093`, `auth-accounts:M-054`
- **Rationale:**
  - A Super Admin creates the account with an unusable password and an OTP of purpose `reset`, and `rbac-v1.yaml:44-54` says the admin finishes through `/auth/password-reset/verify` + `/complete`.
  - The admin app never calls those endpoints, and its login page has no forgot or set-password link (live).
  - A new admin can only get in through a mobile app's forgot-password screen, and cannot recover after the 5-minute code expires.
- **Fix Suggestion:** add a set/forgot-password page (phone → code → new password) on the password-reset endpoints, linked from the login page, with EN + AR copy. Design it on the canvas first (owner rule).

### H3 — Phone numbers are not canonicalized; one handset can hold several accounts (USR-01, AUTH-01)
- **Category:** data / srs-conformance · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** High (reproduced)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/dto/RegisterStartRequestDto.java:8` (pattern `^\+?[0-9]{8,15}$`); `V2__users_auth.sql:20` (UNIQUE on the raw string); `AuthServiceImpl.java:80,114`
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:S-039`, `auth-accounts:S-022`
- **Rationale:**
  - Live: `0933000111`, `+963933000111` and `963933000111` each got `204` and their own register OTP, so they are three identities for one number.
  - This breaks "one account per phone".
  - It weakens the per-phone OTP cap and the GRW-03 self-referral guard.
  - A user who registered in one format and types another at login gets "invalid credentials".
- **Fix Suggestion:** one shared E.164 canonicalizer (default `+963`) applied to every phone input (auth, me, admin, referral), a migration that normalizes and deduplicates existing rows, and the same normalization in the three apps.

### H4 — An admin config edit changes the fare of rides already in progress (UC-A-06, M4-T2 AC)
- **Category:** money / correctness · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** High (reproduced)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/DriverRideServiceImpl.java:251-262`; `RideExtrasCalculator.java:67-117`; `RideLifecycle.regionFee`
- **Claims:** `regions-pricing-surge:U-103`, `regions-pricing-surge:M-073`
- **Rationale:**
  - `complete()` re-reads the service type and region live.
  - Only the surge multiplier and the surcharge percent are locked on the ride, so the wait, pickup-fee and cancellation values are read live as well.
  - Live: an admin raised Comfort `perKmRate` from 3,000 to 30,000 mid-ride, and the completion billed 918 m at the new rate (`distanceFare` 27,540).
  - UC-A-06 says edits apply to new rides only, and `regions-v1.yaml:13-14` promises in-flight rides are not mutated.
  - The M4 AC was passed when no rides existed and was never encoded later.
- **Fix Suggestion:** snapshot the service-type rates and the region extras/cancellation values on the ride at request time (columns or JSONB next to `estimate_components`). Price completion, extras and cancellation from the snapshot, and add a mid-ride config-edit integration test.

### H5 — Incentives and five-star growth let a driver attribute individual ratings (RATE-02, M11 criterion)
- **Category:** privacy · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** High (reproduced)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/IncentiveServiceImpl.java:389-396`; `dto/IncentiveProgressDto.java:43-50` (`ratingCount`); `GrowthServiceImpl.java:90-104` (called from `RatingServiceImpl.java:108`); `apps/driver/src/components/incentives/incentives-screen.tsx:327-339`
- **Claims:** `trust:M-246`, `trust:M-248`, `trust:S-219`, `trust:R-147`
- **Rationale:**
  - Live: `GET /driver/incentives` went from `{average: null, count: 0}`, to `{5.0, 1}` after a 5★ on ride A, to `{3.0, 2}` after a 1★ on ride B. The driver can tell exactly which ride, and so which rider, gave which score.
  - Five-star badges and challenge progress also move at the moment a rating is stored.
  - This breaks the trust-v1 ruling of 2026-10-16 (`trust-v1.yaml:31-39`: no exposed view carries a rating count; the number never moves right after a given ride).
  - Caveat: the owner-approved D15Incentives board itself shows "4.86 average · 21 ratings", so this needs an owner decision, not just a code change.
- **Fix Suggestion:** owner decision. Either show only qualifies/doesn't and the threshold, or feed the weekly view from the nightly snapshot with the ≥ 3-new-ratings rule. Move the five-star badge and challenge evaluation to the nightly job.

### H6 — Driver earnings return 500 once two members of a shared ride have tipped (UC-D-15)
- **Category:** correctness · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** High (reproduced)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/DriverEarningsServiceImpl.java:79-94` (`Collectors.toMap(getRideId, …)` with no merge function), `:115-131`, `:179`
- **Claims:** `money:U-082, M-182`
- **Rationale:**
  - A shared ride legally holds one tip per member (`uq_driver_ledger_ride_tip` on `(ride_id, shared_ride_member_id)`).
  - Live: two member tips (1,000 + 2,000) → `GET /driver/earnings` as driver 007 → 500.
  - With one tip, every per-member fare anchor gets the same ride-level tip and compensation, and `summary()` counts one trip per member, contradicting incentives Q5 (a shared ride is one trip).
  - `SharedRideMoneyIntegrationTest` (3/3 green) never calls the earnings endpoint.
- **Fix Suggestion:** key tips by `(rideId, memberId)` (or group them into lists) and attach each member's own tip; attach the ride-level compensation once; count distinct `rideId`s. Add an earnings/summary step to `SharedRideMoneyIntegrationTest`.

### H7 — A floor-blocked driver can still claim, and be converted onto, scheduled rides (COM-03)
- **Category:** money · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** Medium (behaviour reproduced; the SRS says "offers", so the owner should confirm claims count)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/ClaimBoardServiceImpl.java:220-232` (`dutyAllowed`), `:86`, `:125`; `ScheduledRideClockServiceImpl.java:318-325`
- **Claim:** `money:S-174`
- **Rationale:**
  - Live dispatch excludes floor-blocked drivers through `DriverDutyGates.offersBlockedReason`; the board, the claim and conversion never call it.
  - Live: driver 005 at −5,625 SYP (status `blocked`) saw the ride on the board and claimed it with `200`.
  - The floor exists to stop debt growing, and scheduled cash rides keep growing it.
- **Fix Suggestion:** apply `offersBlockedReason` in `board()` (empty board with a reason), in `claim()` (422 with the floor reason) and at conversion (system release). Confirm the reading with the owner.

### H8 — Admin user search finds no one by name, and finds a phone only through ride history (UC-A-13, ADM-05)
- **Category:** srs-conformance · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** High (observed)
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/pages/money/UserLookup.tsx:56-83`; `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/controller/AdminDirectoryController.java:50-56`
- **Claims:** `analytics-admin:U-114` (search), `analytics-admin:S-251`
- **Rationale:**
  - A UUID opens the user. Anything else goes through the rides search by exact phone; there is no name branch, and `GET /admin/users` accepts only role and date filters.
  - Live: "Dev Rider NoDriver" → "No rides matched this phone".
  - Live: a real user with no rides could not be found by phone.
  - Support cannot find a newly registered user who calls in.
- **Fix Suggestion:** add a `q` filter (name, or normalized phone) to `GET /admin/users` and the contract, a search box on the Users page (canvas first), and route the lookup through it.

### H9 — The rider's tracking map draws no route (TRK-02)
- **Category:** srs-conformance · **Severity:** High · **Certainty:** High (observed)
- **Location:** `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-view.tsx:889-895` (`<RideMap …>` without `route`; the view never calls `getRoute`)
- **Claim:** `dispatch-realtime:S-129`
- **Rationale:**
  - TRK-02: from acceptance the rider sees the driver's live position, **the route** and the ETA.
  - The live position and ETA are there, the route is not: on the in-ride screen the map shows the driver dot and pickup only, while the driver app draws the full route for the same ride.
  - Every rider sees this on every ride.
- **Fix Suggestion:** add a rider route hook mirroring the driver's `use-ride-route.ts` (driver → pickup before boarding, then through the stops to the destination). Pass it as `RideMap`'s `route`, dashed when approximate, and assert it in `ride-flow.test.tsx` (18/18 green today without it).

## Warning
### W1 — Admin shared-ride detail shows the raw key `rideDetail.paymentStatus.split`
- **Category:** i18n-missing-key · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High (live-confirmed)
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/pages/RideDetailPage.tsx:366`
- **Claims:** `live:L1`
- **Rationale:** Every completed shared ride shows a raw i18n key to admins in both languages. The i18n parity test cannot catch it because both files lack the key.
- **Evidence:** PaymentStatus.java:18 has SPLIT("split") (M12 shared rides). admin api/rides.ts:145 type lacks 'split'. en.json:1335-1341 rideDetail.paymentStatus has no split. RideDetailPage.tsx:365-366 renders t(`rideDetail.paymentStatus.${ride.paymentStatus}`). Live screenshot admin-ride-detail-shared-bottom.png shows the raw key.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add 'split' to the PaymentStatus type and EN/AR rideDetail.paymentStatus.split (e.g. 'Paid per member' / AR).

### W2 — Rider app clips names inside short Arabic strings (rating title, “Report {name}”)
- **Category:** i18n-rtl-rendering · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** Medium (live-confirmed)
- **Location:** `apps/rider/src/components/ui/text.tsx:70`
- **Claims:** `live:L2`
- **Rationale:** The defect is real and user-visible: the driver's name is missing from the Arabic rating and report titles. The pattern (the last word, which is the interpolated name, wraps to an undrawn second line) points to Android under-measuring Arabic text in AppText (custom font with no explicit lineHeight / textBreakStrategy). The exact root cause could not be proven statically. The missing avatar may be a separate photo-URL load failure: DriverAvatar (ride-parts.tsx:153-158) renders an <Image> with no fallback when photoUrl is set but fails.
- **Evidence:** Live screenshot rider-rate-shared-ar-no-name.png: the title renders 'كيف كانت رحلتك مع' with no name and an empty 80 px hero above it, even though the line has plenty of width. rating-view.tsx:143-145 renders DriverAvatar + AppText variant=title with {name}. AppText (text.tsx:70-90) sets fontFamily (IBM Plex Sans Arabic; 800 maps to 700Bold, tokens.ts:127), writingDirection rtl and textAlign, but no lineHeight; typography (tokens.ts:132-146) defines no lineHeight. The driver app renders 'الإبلاغ عن Dev' correctly with a Latin name.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Reproduce on device. Try an explicit lineHeight per variant for Arabic and textBreakStrategy='simple' on Android in AppText. Add an onError fallback to initials in DriverAvatar.

### W3 — Driver balance floor and floor-warning margin are not currency-scaled (−50 / 10 in a SYP region)
- **Category:** money-config · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High (live-confirmed)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/MoneyFloors.java:35`
- **Claims:** `live:L4`
- **Rationale:** The dev-seed blocking is covered by launch-checklist row 12. Two parts are not covered: (1) the checklist says to set the floor 'in the admin', yet the admin UI has no field for it, so ops must call the API directly; (2) the 10-unit global warning margin is meaningless in SYP, so COM-03's 'driver warned before blocking' effectively never fires there. No ruling covers either.
- **Evidence:** MoneyFloors.java:35 DEFAULT_FLOOR -50 and :51 null → default. application.yml:164 floor-warning-margin ${MONEY_FLOOR_WARNING_MARGIN:10} is global and currency-agnostic. DevRegionSeeder.java:79 seeds SYP with no floor. launch-checklist.md:19 row 12 and README.md:56 make 'the driver balance floor set in the region's currency scale' an owner launch item. But grep of apps/admin/src finds no driverBalanceFloor editor: regions-v1.yaml:89,620 make it API-only, and BundleForm keeps it when omitted.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add driverBalanceFloor to BundleForm (design first). Make the warning margin per-region or currency-scaled. Seed a SYP-scale floor in DevRegionSeeder.

### W4 — Profile does not show reports the user made or their driver ledger
- **Category:** srs-conformance · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/pages/trust/TrustUi.tsx:52`
- **Claims:** `analytics-admin:U-114`
- **Rationale:** UC-A-13 lists 'driver ledger (if driver), reports (made/received)' on the profile. Reports the user made and their driver ledger are not on the profile. The ledger can still be reached through the Driver ledgers lookup, so the impact is partial: Warning.
- **Evidence:** (UC-A-13b claim) UserProfileTabs (:52-60) shows only the trust, wallet and history tabs. UserTrustPage uses useReportsAgainst (reports against the user). AdminReportsController.list (:44-51) filters only by status and reportedUserId, with no reporter filter, and the AdminUserTrust schema (trust-v1.yaml:896-908) has no reports-made data. The only links to /driver-ledgers/:id are in UserLookup (ledger mode) and the LiveOps driver click, never from the user profile.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add a reporterUserId filter to GET /admin/reports and a 'Reports made' section on the trust tab. For driver accounts with settlements:view, add a driver-ledger tab or link on the profile, on the canvas first.

### W5 — Deactivated admin signs in to an empty shell with no reason shown
- **Category:** auth-ux · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/auth/permissions.ts:17`
- **Claims:** `app-admin:1`
- **Rationale:** USR-06 (SRS.md:98) says whole-account deactivation keeps login working 'to show the reason'. The admin app never shows a reason, and a deactivated admin sees misleading 'No access' pages. API access is still cut, so this is not a security issue. The attached live evidence (SOS panel) is unrelated to this claim.
- **Evidence:** AuthServiceImpl.java:285-291 holdsRoleForClient checks only role presence, not state, so a deactivated admin (rbac-v1.yaml:104-112: role → suspended) logs in. permissions.ts:17-19 gives an empty set for a non-active admin assignment. api/auth.ts:11-22 RoleAssignment drops suspensionReason/Note. There is no deactivated screen, only the generic ForbiddenPage ('Your role does not include permission…').
- **Fix Suggestion:** Show a dedicated 'access deactivated' screen with the reason and logout when the admin assignment or user state is suspended (design first), or have the owner rule that the backend refuses admin login.

### W6 — Admin push tap never routes to live ops (background tap opens nothing)
- **Category:** notifications · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/notifications/deep-links.ts:18`
- **Claims:** `app-admin:3`
- **Rationale:** A background web-push tap on the safety-critical SOS alert closes the notification and opens nothing. Even the documented intent (open the center) is not met. The in-tab SosAlertsProvider sticky toast mitigates this while a tab is open.
- **Evidence:** ADMIN_SCREENS maps only 'notifications' (:18-20). NotificationRenderer.java:869-882 sends the SOS push with link null; its Javadoc says 'a push tap opens the notification center'. FcmPushSender.sendChunk sets no WebpushConfig/fcmOptions.link. public/firebase-messaging-sw.js registers no notificationclick handler. Firebase SW onNotificationClick (index.sw.esm.js:1443-1446) returns without opening a window when getLink() is null.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Give the SOS admin push a link and set webpush fcmOptions.link (same origin, e.g. the live-ops URL) in FcmPushSender for admin tokens. Map the screen in ADMIN_SCREENS.

### W7 — No in-app language switch for signed-out users, applicants or suspended drivers
- **Category:** i18n · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/driver/src/app/_layout.tsx:64`
- **Claims:** `app-driver:2`
- **Rationale:** Applicants (through the multi-step wizard) and role-suspended drivers cannot reach the NFR-I1 settings switch at all. Signed-out users get the device language. The approved boards (DriverSuspended) show no toggle either, so a design step is needed. The rider app keeps Settings reachable when suspended.
- **Evidence:** _layout.tsx:64-69 declares settings only under Stack.Protected guard={signedIn && isDriver}. The applicant stack (:148-162) and the driverTools stack (:168-216) have no language control. apply-to-drive-screen.tsx gate (incl. RoleSuspendedGate :252+) offers only logout. Language falls back to the device locale (i18n/index.ts:71).
- **Fix Suggestion:** With owner and board approval, expose the language option on the gate/applicant screens, or move settings into the signedIn stack.

### W8 — Driver auth flows not at parity with rider
- **Category:** parity · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/driver/src/components/auth/error-key.ts:9`

### W9 — Last-Super-Admin guard can be bypassed
- **Category:** concurrency · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** Medium
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/AdminAccountServiceImpl.java:250`
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:M-064`
- **Rationale:** An edge case: it needs a race or a deliberate self-deletion. It is recoverable, because re-registering the phone reactivates the account with its roles (USR-07). Still, M3 MC-2 last-Super-Admin protection is not airtight.
- **Evidence:** requireNotLastActiveSuperAdmin (AdminAccountServiceImpl.java:250-261) counts ACTIVE Super Admin assignments with a plain count. It takes no lock, RoleAssignment has no @Version, and it ignores users.state. Two Super Admins deactivating each other concurrently both pass the check. MeServiceImpl.deleteAccount (:161-175) has no admin guard, so the sole Super Admin can soft-delete their own account while the assignment still counts as active.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Lock the Super Admin assignments (SELECT … FOR UPDATE) before counting, count only users in state active, and block deletion of the last active Super Admin.

### W10 — Admin-role deactivation/reactivation sends no notification
- **Category:** srs-gap · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** Medium
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/AdminAccountServiceImpl.java:142`
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:S-047`
- **Rationale:** Role-level suspension of the admin role is an account state change under USR-04. M3 predated the notification seam and nobody went back to it. Impact is limited to admins.
- **Evidence:** deactivate and reactivate (AdminAccountServiceImpl.java:139-180) only flip the role-assignment state and write an audit record; NotificationService is not used. USR-04 (SRS.md:96) says every state change notifies the user with the reason. rbac-v1.yaml:104-118 has no reason field and no notification note, and no ruling exempts admin roles.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Queue an account-category notification on admin deactivate and reactivate (an optional reason/note on the endpoint), or record an owner ruling that exempts admin roles.

### W11 — Driver app shows generic error when the SMS gateway fails
- **Category:** copy · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/driver/src/components/auth/register-screen.tsx:69`
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:U-144`
- **Rationale:** The driver app shows generic error copy where UC-S-01 E4 requires specific copy. This is a copy and parity gap, not a broken flow.
- **Evidence:** register-screen.tsx:69-74 maps only {409, 429}. error-key.ts:9 falls back to 'auth.errors.generic' for any other ApiError, including a 502 from the SMS gateway. The rider app maps this case to auth.otpSendFailed (rider register-screen.tsx:106-113). UC-S-01 E4 (shared.md:30) requires the copy 'code could not be sent, try again later'.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Map 502 to a new auth.otp.sendFailed key (EN and AR) in the driver register, forgot-password, 2FA and phone-change screens.

### W12 — Phone held by a deleted account fails only at the final step
- **Category:** edge-case · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/MeServiceImpl.java:232`
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:U-163`
- **Rationale:** The documented start check lets the user through a flow that can never succeed. This is an edge case that wastes two OTPs out of the hourly cap.
- **Evidence:** assertPhoneFree (MeServiceImpl.java:232-240) filters out DELETED holders, which matches UC-S-06 step 2 and contract auth-v1.yaml:264 ('active/suspended'). But V2__users_auth.sql:20 uq_users_phone is a full unique constraint, and no later migration makes it partial. phoneChangeComplete (:148-155) therefore always fails with 409 after both OTPs are spent.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Reject a deleted-held phone at start with a distinct 409, or get an owner decision on releasing deleted holders' phones.

### W13 — Soft delete skips preconditions and leaves pending activity alive
- **Category:** srs-gap · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High (live-confirmed)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/MeServiceImpl.java:160`
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:U-165`
- **Rationale:** Partial: matching already excludes deleted drivers, but the rider's own pending requests stay alive and the online flag survives. The flag then shows as 'online since…' after reactivation.
- **Evidence:** deleteAccount (MeServiceImpl.java:160-175) withdraws only the driver application, revokes sessions and devices, and clears the phone-change session. It never resets driver_profiles.is_online or cancels the rider's pre-acceptance requests. Compare SuspensionServiceImpl.java:257-272 cancelPendingRequests and :296-309 forceOfflineUnlessOnRide. Partial mitigation: RideRepository.java:213 findDispatchCandidates joins users u.state='active', so a deleted driver is never offered rides. LIVE: is_online persisted across delete and reactivate.
- **Fix Suggestion:** After the E1/E2 gates, reuse the suspension cleanup: cancel the pending request with its offer withdrawn, run the scheduled/shared hooks, and set the driver offline.

### W14 — Driver and admin language switches are not saved to the account; pushes and the notification center stay in the old language
- **Category:** i18n · srs-gap · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/driver/src/components/settings-screen.tsx:176`; `apps/driver/src/components/settings-screen.tsx:181`; `apps/admin/src/layout/LanguageSwitcher.tsx:17`
- **Claims:** `auth-accounts:U-172`, `app-driver:2`, `app-admin:2`
- **Rationale:** After a settings switch, the driver's and admin's account language drifts from the UI, so pushes and SMS go out in the old language. A workaround exists for the driver through profile edit, so this is partial.
  - UC-S-10 (use-cases/shared.md:109) says the settings switch 'persists to the account and applies to notifications and SMS templates'. The driver settings switch does not, so pushes, the center and SMS stay in the old language unless the driver saves profile-edit. The rider app shows the intended beh…
  - UC-S-10 requires the switch to persist to the account. An admin who switches to Arabic keeps getting EN SOS pushes and center rows, and the admin app has no profile editor.
- **Evidence:** The driver settings OptionGroup calls LocaleProvider.setLanguage (LocaleProvider.tsx:77-86), which only stores the language on the device. The admin LanguageSwitcher.tsx calls only i18n.changeLanguage, and i18n.ts:23-25 persists to localStorage. The rider settings-screen switchLanguage PUTs /users/me. The driver profile-edit-screen.tsx:47-60 can save the account language, but the settings switch does not. UC-S-10 (shared.md:109) says the preference persists to the account.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Have the driver settings switch (and the admin switcher) also PUT /users/me {name, language} best-effort, as the rider app does.

### W15 — Admin rides screen lacks kind filter and related reports/tickets
- **Category:** admin-ui · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/api/rides.ts:71-95`
- **Claims:** `comms-safety:U-120`
- **Rationale:** UC-A-18 (docs/use-cases/admin.md:122-124) lists the kind filter and 'related reports/tickets/SOS' on the detail. Both are missing in the admin UI, and no ruling defers them (the M10 backlog mentions only SOS filters).
- **Evidence:** AdminRidesController.search accepts kind (AdminRidesController.java:48-57), but searchAdminRides (apps/admin/src/api/rides.ts:71-95) never sets kind, and RidesPage has no kind control. RideDetailPage.tsx has no reports or ticket section (grep finds no 'report' or 'ticket'). rides-v1.yaml AdminRideDetail says 'reports/tickets/SOS join the detail when their milestones land', but reports (M11) and tickets (M14) never joined.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add a kind select that passes ?kind= through searchAdminRides. Add a 'Related' section on the ride detail listing the ride's reports and support or lost-item tickets with links (backend join plus UI). Design it first and confirm scope with the owner.

### W16 — The admin app cannot open or show the SOS-linked support ticket (UC-A-20 step 3, support ruling 9)
- **Category:** admin-ui · missing-ui · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High (live-confirmed)
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/api/comms.ts:37-49`; `apps/admin/src/api/comms.ts:37`; `apps/admin/src/pages/liveops/SosAlertPanel.tsx:239`
- **Claims:** `comms-safety:U-122`, `support:M-294`, `support:R-131`, `app-admin:1`
- **Rationale:** UC-A-20 step 3 ('records actions in a linked ticket/note') and the M14 scope ('SOS resolution notes upgrade to real linked tickets', M14-support.md:12) are met only in the backend. Admins cannot open the linked ticket. The acknowledge note partly satisfies 'note'. No deferral was found in the backlog lists.
  - Ruling 9 in the support-v1 header (lines 46-48) says an admin opens the SOS-linked ticket 'from the acknowledge flow', and M14 says the SOS note stub 'upgrade[s] to real linked tickets'. Admins have no way to use this rail from the dashboard. The backend side of the M14-T2 AC is met, so the gap is…
  - This is the same root cause as support:M-294, seen from the ruling side. Ruling 9's admin flow does not exist in the admin UI.
  - This is real: a backend feature from an M14 deliverable with no admin entry point. UC-A-20 step 3 allows 'a linked ticket/note', and the acknowledge note does exist. SAFE-01 alerting works. So this is a partial gap, not a missing core requirement.
- **Evidence:** The backend exposes POST /api/v1/admin/sos-events/{sosEventId}/ticket (AdminCommsController.java:122-130). support-v1.yaml ruling (9) says that 'from the acknowledge flow an admin opens a ticket on behalf of the SOS caller', and comms-v1.yaml points the acknowledge flow at this rail. No non-test code in apps/admin/src calls it: grep for sos-events shows only SosAlertsProvider polling and tests. SosAlertPanel.tsx offers acknowledge only.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add openSosTicket in admin/src/api/comms.ts and ticketId to AdminSosEvent. Add an 'Open linked ticket' action, or a link to /support/{ticketId}, in SosAlertPanel and RideDetailPage for support:handle. Map 409 ticket_already_linked. Design it first.

### W20 — Arabic ride-flow amounts show 'SYP' instead of 'ل.س'
- **Category:** i18n-currency · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High (live-confirmed)

The M8 backlog item 'M7 driver formatter retrofit' (README.md:96) covers Western digits only, not the currency label, so no ruling covers this. Arabic drivers see the Latin code on the offer, to-pickup summary, claim board and shared split.
- **Evidence:** rides/format.ts:32-42 formatMoney -> t('ride.money',{currency,amount}); driver ar.json:502 "money": "{{amount}} {{currency}}" interpolates the raw code. Used by offer-card.tsx:101, ride-sheets.tsx:147, shared-parts.tsx:394/527/533. money/format.ts currencyLabel() maps SYP->'ل.س' but is not used here. __tests__/ride-rtl.test.tsx:63 asserts / SYP$/. LIVE CONFIRMED driver-offer-ar.png shows '21,307 SYP'.

### W21 — Arabic availability window ranges are not LTR-isolated and are likely to render reversed
- **Category:** rtl-bidi · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** Medium
- **Location:** `apps/driver/src/components/scheduled/availability-screen.tsx:65`
- **Claims:** `design-driver-b:D12AvailabilityAr`
- **Rationale:** Under the bidi algorithm, the en dash between two digit runs resolves to R in an RTL paragraph, so the next-day variant (which has strong Arabic) will display as '02:00–22:00'. The plain chip is likely reversed too under RTL base direction. Other driver surfaces use isolateDigits, and no ruling was found.
- **Evidence:** windowLabel returns `${window.startsAt}–${window.endsAt}` (availability-screen.tsx:60-66), rendered raw or in t('scheduled.availability.withNextDay') = '{{range}} · حتى اليوم التالي' at :290-292, with no isolate. Board D12AvailabilityAr wraps the range in <span dir="ltr">. The AR test (scheduled-rides.test.tsx:362) checks only the text content, not visual order.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Wrap the range with isolateDigits(label, language) in windowLabel (and the next-day variant); add an AR test asserting the isolate characters.

### W22 — Scheduled release / rider-busy notices are generic: no time, no name and no 'counts as a release' consequence

- **Rationale:** The notice is generic: no time, no rider name, and no 'counts as a release' consequence. The claim's dismiss point is overstated, because the banner does have a Dismiss TextButton; it also auto-clears after 8 s. Copy and design drift, no ruling.
- **Evidence:** RideNoticeBanner (ride-parts.tsx:344-366) renders message=t(`ride.notice.${notice}`) with no title. en scheduledReleased = 'A scheduled ride claim was released. It's back on the board or in live dispatch.' Board D12Notices: 'Your 09:00 claim was released \| ... It counts as a release.' Auto-clear NOTICE_MS = 8000 (DriverRideProvider.tsx:180).
- **Fix Suggestion:** Carry the ride time and name from the push payload into a titled notice and add the consequence line, or record an amendment.

On settlement, noticeForEndedRide returns 'sharedSettled' (DriverRideProvider.tsx:224) and home takes over. ### W24 — Settings shows a debug 'Layout direction' row and an API-version line that no board has
- **Category:** design-drift · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High

- **Rationale:** This is M1 scaffolding: review-M01.md:131 praised a JS direction flag as interim feedback. It is obsolete now because switchLanguage reloads the app on a direction change (settings-screen.tsx:63). Users see an untranslated debug token on a screen the owner approved without it. No ruling covers it. The fault is design and copy drift, not broken behaviour, so it stays a Warning.

The RiderSettingsRides board has neither. The live run showed 'اتجاه الواجهة RTL' (rider-settings-ar.png).

### W25 — Shared member on-the-way sheet shows the whole-ride estimate instead of 'your seats · share of the final fare'
- **Category:** design-drift · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High

- **Claims:** `design-rider-b:RiderSharedOnWay`
- **Rationale:** A shared member is shown the whole group's fare as their estimate. That misleading money display goes beyond cosmetic drift, so this is a Warning.

EstimateFooter (ride-parts.tsx:293-321) prints ride.estimate.components.total as '{{money}} est.'. For a shared ride that is the whole-ride estimate (scheduled-shared-v1.yaml:940, 'estimateTotal: Whole-ride estimate'). The only shared-specific element is sharedChip (:178-188). The RiderSharedOnWay board shows 'Meet at <pickup>' and 'Your 2 seats · Cash / share of the final fare'. No ruling was found in review-M12.md or the M12 docs.
- **Fix Suggestion:** For kind=shared, replace the EstimateFooter amount with 'Your {party} seats · {payment}' plus 'share of the final fare' (new EN/AR keys), and add the 'Meet at' pickup row (accepted and arrived sheets).

### W26 — Accept does not re-check role ACTIVE/online; open offer survives suspension or going offline
- **Category:** edge-case · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/DriverRideServiceImpl.java:110`
- **Claims:** `dispatch-realtime:R-099`
- **Rationale:** A suspended or just-offline driver can still accept an offer already open, within its 15 s window. This is real, but the window is narrow.
- **Evidence:** accept() (DriverRideServiceImpl.java:110-142) calls only rideAccess.requireDriver, which checks application APPROVED and that a DRIVER role row exists (RideAccess.java:62-71), not role state or is_online. forceOfflineUnlessOnRide (SuspensionServiceImpl.java:296-309) and DriverAvailabilityServiceImpl.set(false) (:47-64) never withdraw the driver's open offer. withdrawOpenOffer is called only for rider cancel, account-suspension cancel of the rider's own request, shared and scheduled. rides-v1.yaml:100-101 says a suspended driver is never offered.
- **Fix Suggestion:** In accept(), require the driver role ACTIVE and profile.isOnline (409 offer_closed). On a forced or voluntary offline, withdraw that driver's open offer with an offer.withdrawn frame so dispatch advances.

### W27 — A shared-ride member's promo discount is not itemized in any breakdown the rider sees
- **Category:** srs-conformance · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/DriverRideServiceImpl.java:306`
- **Claims:** `growth:S-178,`
- **Rationale:** GRW-01 says the discount is 'itemized in breakdown'. A shared-ride member who used a promo sees a share lower than per-seat × party with no explanation. No ruling covers how the discount is displayed: ruling 2 covers only how the discount is applied.
- **Evidence:** DriverRideServiceImpl.complete: the fare-level applyDiscount runs only for non-SHARED rides (:265). On a shared ride the member's discount is subtracted from shareAmount (:306) and passed only to rideMoneyService.applySharedAtCompletion. RiderRideServiceImpl.receipt returns the whole-ride FinalFareDto (discount 0) plus memberMoney. ReceiptDto has no member-discount field. apps/rider/src/screens/shared/shared-split.tsx shows seats, per-seat amount and 'Your share' with no discount line, and ride-format.ts:263 reads only fare.discount.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Expose the member's applied discount (from promo_redemptions, or a member column) in the shared member view and the receipt money. Render a 'Promo discount' line in SharedSplit or the receipt in EN and AR, after a canvas check per the design-first rule.

### W28 — No grant/refund entry point from the admin ride detail
- **Category:** admin-ui · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/pages/RideDetailPage.tsx:92`
- **Claims:** `money:M-184`
- **Rationale:** UC-A-15 (docs/use-cases/admin.md:102-104) only requires the profile or ticket entry points, and those exist. The M8-T5 ticket's 'ride detail' entry point is missing, which forces the admin to look the rider up separately. Partial, with no ruling.
- **Evidence:** M08-money.md (M8-T5) says 'From user profile / ride detail: grant credit, issue refund'. RideDetailPage.tsx contains only the /rides link (:92), tel: links (:223,:234) and the live-ops link (:295). It has no wallet, grant or refund action and not even a link to the rider's profile. GrantRefundPanel exists on UserWalletPage and is reachable from support tickets.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add a 'Refund rider' action on RideDetailPage, gated by wallets:manage, that deep-links to /users/{riderUserId}/wallet with refund and rideId pre-selected (EN+AR). Design it first per CLAUDE.md.

### W29 — Zero-total cash ride still asks the driver to collect SYP 0 and cash-confirm
- **Category:** money-ux · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/DriverRideServiceImpl.java:326`
- **Claims:** `money:U-021`
- **Rationale:** This is an edge case. A fully discounted cash ride asks the driver to collect SYP 0 and confirm cash, and the rider push says to pay in cash. The confusing UX is real, but it is not stuck: cash-confirm works.
- **Evidence:** For non-shared rides, DriverRideServiceImpl.java:326 always sets PaymentStatus.PENDING. The wallet auto-confirm only applies to wallet-method rides that are fully covered (RideMoneyServiceImpl walletStep:187-188). On a cash-method ride with total 0, the cash provider collects 0, so no crash, but the status stays pending. The driver CompleteScreen shows 'Collect in cash' with collect = cashDue or the fare total (apps/driver/src/components/ride/complete-screen.tsx:132-176) and needs a cash-confirm. No ruling on zero-total rides was found in the milestones, growth-v1 or money-v1. The live eviden…
- **Fix Suggestion:** When total == 0 and no cash dues are earmarked, settle at completion (paid_cash or paid_wallet with paymentConfirmedAt), skip the collect screen, and use 'nothing to pay' copy in EN and AR.

### W30 — Mark-unpaid has no support path behind it
- **Category:** support-flow · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/DriverRideServiceImpl.java:492-515`
- **Claims:** `money:U-022, U-073`
- **Rationale:** The use case is only partly met. A manual ticket path exists since M14, but nothing makes support aware of an unpaid ride, and the copy promises follow-up that never happens automatically. No ruling covers it.
- **Evidence:** settle() (DriverRideServiceImpl.java:492-515) sets UNPAID, releases carried dues and emits ride.updated. It creates no ticket, flag or admin signal. UC-D-09 E1 (docs/use-cases/driver.md:90) says 'driver marks unpaid → support path', and the rider UC (docs/use-cases/rider.md:79) says 'support ticket path'. The driver copy says 'Marked unpaid — support follows up' (apps/driver/src/i18n/translations/en.json:679). The admin rides search has no payment-status filter (AdminRidesController.java:48-57). A manual path does exist: the driver can open a support ticket linked to a ride (apps/driver/src/c…
- **Fix Suggestion:** Ask the owner. Options: auto-open a support ticket on mark-unpaid, add an admin rides filter paymentStatus=unpaid, or deep-link the driver into ticket creation pre-filled with the ride. Or change the copy.

### W31 — Expiry reminders state the tier (30/7) as days left, not the real days left
- **Category:** copy · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/DocumentReminderWriter.java:51`
- **Claims:** `onboarding:R-063`
- **Rationale:** A document first caught inside the window (e.g. approved 20 or 3 days out, or a missed job run) gets a reminder saying '30 days left' or '7 days left'. The expiry date shown next to it is correct, so this is a misleading-copy edge case.
- **Evidence:** DocumentExpiryServiceImpl.java:48-63 computes the real daysLeft but passes only the tier, and DocumentReminderWriter.java:51 queues 'daysLeft', daysLeftTier. NotificationRenderer.java:1046-1060 renders '%s expires on %s (%s left)', and its daysLeft<=0 branch can never be reached. Contract onboarding-v1.yaml:67 names the param 'daysLeft 30\|7', so the param matches the contract, but the rendered copy claims it is the time remaining.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Pass the real days left as the param, keep the tier only for choosing the stamp, update the onboarding-v1.yaml:67 note, and add unit cases for 20 and 3 days.

### W32 — The rider app never shows the vehicle photo (DRV-03, UC-R-05)
- **Category:** srs-gap · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High (live-confirmed)
- **Location:** `apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-parts.tsx:195`
- **Claims:** `onboarding:S-196`, `onboarding:R-062`, `rides-core:U-013`
- **Rationale:** An SRS item is not shown, but the rest of the identity block is there and the owner-approved board omits the photo too, so this is partial rather than High. It needs a design step first.
  - Same root cause as S-196.
  - A duplicate root of S-196.
- **Evidence:** DriverCard (ride-parts.tsx:195-245) renders DriverAvatar(driver.photoUrl), the make/model/color text and the plate. Grep finds no vehicle.photoUrl use in apps/rider/src. The backend sends it (rides-v1.yaml:202-203, 'vehicle make/model/color/plate + first photo'). DRV-03 (SRS.md:194) and UC-R-05 step 2 list the vehicle photo. The approved board RiderDriverOnWay.dc.html also has no vehicle photo (text: 'SK 4.9 ★ Samer Silver Hyundai Accent دمشق 502345'). LIVE CONFIRMED.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add the vehicle photo to the board and get it approved, then render vehicle.photoUrl in DriverCard (resolved and refetched on expiry) with a test.

### W33 — Admin theme contains hardcoded colours
- **Category:** design-system · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/theme/global.css:2400`
- **Claims:** `platform-nfr:M-012`
- **Rationale:** The literal M1-T3 acceptance criterion is violated by later additions (M10 SOS, M11 roles, M15 charts). Values that duplicate tokens show this is drift, not an approved exception; the owner-approved safety red should still be a token. No visual defect, so Warning, not higher.
- **Evidence:** global.css has 27 raw hex literals. Some duplicate existing tokens exactly: :2400-2401 `background: #b42318` = --labby-danger (tokens.css:69); :2351 `#fef3f2` = --labby-danger-bg (tokens.css:70). Others have no token at all (:3027 #e7b37a, :3386 #e3d3f0, :3647 #c9a8e3). M1-T3 AC: 'theme uses token variables (no hardcoded colors)'.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Replace #b42318/#fef3f2 with var(--labby-danger)/var(--labby-danger-bg). Add tokens for safety red, its tints, the suspended amber, the lilac chart/trend tints and the dark hover, then reference them.

### W34 — Fresh clone cannot boot backend with documented commands (JWT_SECRET has no default)
- **Category:** dev-experience · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/resources/application.yml:63`
- **Claims:** `platform-nfr:M-020`
- **Rationale:** M1 milestone AC (M01-foundation.md: 'One git clone + documented commands bring up backend + all three clients locally') fails on a fresh clone: following the documented commands, Spring cannot resolve ${JWT_SECRET} and the backend does not boot. The no-default choice is deliberate and correct (security), but the docs and make target were not updated to match. Only the developer flow is affected, so Warning.
- **Evidence:** application.yml:60-63 `jwt-secret: ${JWT_SECRET}` (no default, comment says boot fails fast); application-dev.yml sets only log levels; backend/Makefile:26-27 `run: SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=dev $(MVN) spring-boot:run` with no .env sourcing; pom.xml:224-239 spring-boot-maven-plugin has no env config; no spring.config.import/dotenv anywhere. backend/README.md:35-37 and .env.example:2-4 both say every variable has a working default, but .env.example:53 contradicts it ('REQUIRED: the app has no fallback'). Root README.md:33-35 says just `make -C backend db-up` then `make -C backend run`.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Have `make run` load backend/.env if present (`set -a; [ -f .env ] && . ./.env; set +a`) and add an explicit 'cp .env.example .env and set JWT_SECRET' step to backend/README.md and the root README. Correct the 'every variable has a default' wording in README.md:35-37 and .env.example:2-4. Keep no committed default.

### W35 — Most screens hold server state ad hoc instead of domain facades
- **Category:** structure · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/pages/FeedbackPage.tsx:70`
- **Claims:** `platform-nfr:S-328`
- **Rationale:** CLAUDE.md:18 explicitly names 'facades for state' as a principle the React/RN apps must translate. The per-domain api/*.ts modules give a service layer, but data and mutation state is not encapsulated per domain, so the principle is only partly applied. Design and structure drift with no user-facing defect, so Warning.
- **Evidence:** FeedbackPage.tsx:5-15 imports listAdminFeedback/getFeedbackStats/setFeedbackTags from ../api/support. :70-80 keeps result/stats/errors/attempts in local useState, and :82+ fetches in useEffect with a cancelled flag. Most pages under apps/admin/src/pages follow this pattern. Hook-style facades exist only in a few places (surge, liveops, rider ride context).
- **Fix Suggestion:** Introduce per-domain hooks (e.g. useFeedback(query) returning data/error/retry/mutations) for new and touched pages, and migrate the rest incrementally. Or record an owner ruling that page-local state over typed api modules is the accepted translation.

### W36 — No lint-enforced module boundaries or architecture rule in the three clients
- **Category:** structure · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/eslint.config.js:8`; `apps/rider/package.json:12`; `apps/rider/eslint.config.js:5`; `apps/driver/eslint.config.js:5`
- **Claims:** `platform-nfr:S-333`, `platform-nfr:S-340`, `platform-nfr:M-021`, `app-rider:6`, `app-driver:6`, `app-admin:6`
- **Rationale:** Explicit project principle with no frontend implementation. review-M15.md:278 lists it as 'accepted in earlier audits', but I found no earlier audit or owner ruling that accepts it (review-M01 to M14 and the tracker have no match), so it is not covered. No user-facing impact, so Warning.
  - Same root cause as S-333, which should be the primary finding; this one is a duplicate under the shared-pattern requirement id STR-S02. No ruling covers it.
  - Lint and test gates exist and fail on violations, but no frontend gate checks architecture, so the 'architecture' part of the AC is backend-only. This duplicates S-333 under the M1 milestone criterion. The M1 audit's 'met' was an auditor judgement, not an owner ruling.
  - This is a real structure deviation. The M15 review only notes it as accepted. No owner ruling or amendment records the waiver, so it is not covered.
  - This is a real deviation from the structure rule, and no recorded ruling waives it.
  - This is a real structure deviation with layer inversions (a provider imports from pages), and no ruling waives it.
- **Evidence:** apps/admin/eslint.config.js:8-26 uses only js/tseslint/prettier/react-hooks/react-refresh. apps/rider/eslint.config.js and apps/driver/eslint.config.js use only eslint-config-expo/flat. No eslint-plugin-boundaries, no-restricted-imports, import/no-restricted-paths, dependency-cruiser or madge in any app (searched the configs and src). CLAUDE.md:18 names 'enforced module boundaries' as a principle to translate; M01-foundation.md user story: 'architecture rules are enforced by tooling (ArchUnit, lint)'.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add no-restricted-imports zones or eslint-plugin-boundaries per client: in admin, pages may import api/ui/auth but api must not import pages/ui; in rider/driver, domain folders must not import screens/components. Wire into the existing `npm run check`. Alternatively, record an owner ruling accepting convention-only boundaries.

### W37 — Admin UI does not name the surge zones a boundary edit would leave outside
- **Category:** ux-error-handling · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/admin/src/pages/regions/BundleForm.tsx:44`
- **Claims:** `regions-pricing-surge:U-104`
- **Rationale:** UC-A-06 E1 (owner ruling 2026-10-07, admin.md:51) requires the refusal to name the zones. The backend names them, but the admin never sees the names, so they cannot tell which zones to move or delete.
- **Evidence:** mapSaveError (:44-54) maps every 409 to 'regionDetail.errors.saveConflict'. In en.json:371 that is one generic sentence listing four possible causes. RegionServiceImpl.requireZonesInside throws a ConflictException whose message names the zones, with no code or details, and the UI never shows that message.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Give each region 409 a distinct code (zones_outside_boundary with details.zoneNames, boundary_overlap, region_name_taken, active_unfill). Map them in BundleForm to localized EN/AR messages, interpolating the zone names.

### W38 — Disabling a service type with claimed scheduled rides gives no warning (UC-A-07 E1)
- **Category:** srs-conformance · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High (live-confirmed)
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/ServiceTypeServiceImpl.java:65`
- **Claims:** `regions-pricing-surge:U-106`, `regions-pricing-surge:M-074`
- **Rationale:** The UC-A-07 E1 warning is missing, and the M12 deferral was never honoured. Claimed scheduled rides still run: ScheduledRideServiceImpl checks isEnabled only at schedule() time (:68), which matches UC-A-07's 'existing rides unaffected'. The impact is therefore that the admin is not told about the affected rides. No ride breaks and no money moves, so Warning rather than High.
  - The AC 'UC-A-05…07 exceptions covered' is now partial, because the deferral target (M12) has passed. Permission gating and auditing are present.
- **Evidence:** updateServiceType (:65-86) applies the request, enabled flag included, with no scheduled-ride check. ServiceTypeService.java javadoc and regions-v1.yaml:182-185 still say the E1 warning comes 'once scheduled rides exist (M12)'. review-M04.md:28 records that E1 was 'explicitly deferred to M12', and nothing in M12 picked it up. LIVE CONFIRMED (probe6): PUT enabled:false returned 200 with no warning while a claimed scheduled ride existed.
- **Fix Suggestion:** In updateServiceType, when enabled goes from true to false, query scheduled rides in state scheduled with a claimed driver on this type. If any exist, return 409 service_type_has_claimed_rides with the ride list unless an acknowledge flag is sent. Update regions-v1, the admin ServiceTypesSection, and add an integration test.

### W39 — A suspended rider's booking refusal shows the raw suspension-reason enum and no appeal/contact info
- **Category:** copy · i18n-copy · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/rider/src/ride/ride-errors.ts:26`
- **Claims:** `rides-core:U-011`, `app-rider:1`
- **Rationale:** Raw enum text in user copy (edge path: the home screen normally shows the suspended screen instead) and no appeal or support entry. The board's footer gives only 'reactivated if our team reviews it'.
  - A raw English enum token (e.g. "(low_rating)") is shown to users, in Arabic too. The 403 also raises the suspension signal (auth/suspension-signal.ts), so home switches to the suspended screen. Non-home screens such as schedule and shared-create still show the raw text. No ruling covers this.
- **Evidence:** ride-errors.ts:26-30 interpolates the raw suspensionReason into booking.errors.rider_suspended_reason, giving 'Your rider account is suspended ({{reason}}).' (ride-translations.ts:87-88, AR :366-367), e.g. '(low_rating)' in both languages. suspended-screen.tsx:7 says 'No "Contact support" action (support arrives in M14)'. M14 has shipped, but no appeal entry was added. UC-R-04 E3 (rider.md:42) requires the reason plus appeal info.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Map suspensionReason to localized labels in ride-errors.ts, and add a contact-support/appeal action to the suspended screen after a board update.

### W40 — No notifications for ETA-affecting events (reroute / heavy delay)
- **Category:** srs-gap · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/RideTrackingServiceImpl.java:129`
- **Claims:** `rides-core:U-017`
- **Rationale:** A secondary UC step is missing. The live ETA is shown, so the core flow is fine.
- **Evidence:** RideTrackingServiceImpl.check (:107-147) only recomputes etaSeconds, targeting destination or pickup and ignoring stops (:136-141). NotificationEvent has no reroute or delay event. UC-R-06 step 3 (rider.md:63) says ETA-affecting events surface as notifications. Grep finds no deferral in milestones or contracts.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Detect a significant ETA increase in the monitor and queue a ride-category notice with EN/AR templates, or record an owner deferral. Include the remaining stops in the ETA target.

### W41 — Ride history has no filters (UC-R-16 'list with filters')
- **Category:** srs-gap · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/controller/RiderRidesController.java:92`
- **Claims:** `rides-core:U-040`
- **Rationale:** A UC detail is missing. The approved board omits it too, and the history list otherwise works.
- **Evidence:** RiderRidesController.java:92-98 history() takes only page and size. ride-history-screen.tsx has no filter state. UC-R-16 (rider.md:157) says 'list with filters'. The approved board RiderHistory.dc.html shows a plain list without filters. No ruling drops the filters.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Get an owner ruling dropping filters for V1, or design filter chips on the canvas and add state/date query params to GET /rides and the contract.

### W42 — Driver is not told when the system releases their claim at conversion (counted in the metric); rider copy misleading
- **Category:** notifications · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `backend/src/main/java/com/labby/backend/service/ScheduledRideSupport.java:155-158`
- **Claims:** `scheduled-shared:R-109`
- **Rationale:** scheduled-shared-v1.yaml:148-161 (ruling 2026-10-16) defines the release and its metric effect but does not say the driver must be told. Still, a release that counts against DRV-06 happens silently, and the client code built to receive that push never fires. The rider text 'stays open for other drivers' is loosely accurate even when the ride moves to live dispatch. This is an edge case and gap, not a requirement users hit routinely.
- **Evidence:** ScheduledRideSupport.release pushes SCHEDULED_RELEASED only to ride.getRiderUserId() (lines ~155-158), and the renderer audience is RIDER (NotificationRenderer.java:387-399). ScheduledRideClockServiceImpl.java:188-198 releases an offline or busy claimer with OTHER/unavailable_at_conversion and sends the driver nothing. Only the vehicle_changed branch (:183-186) pushes SCHEDULED_CLAIM_LOST to the driver. The driver app handles 'scheduled.released' as 'a claim given back by the system' (apps/driver/src/notifications/deep-links.ts:148-155; en.json:633), so that code never runs.
- **Fix Suggestion:** On a system release at conversion, also push scheduled.released, or a driver-audience variant, to the claimer with EN+AR driver copy (owner proofread). Optionally use separate rider copy for a T−lead fallback.

### W43 — The driver can report a rider after the ride only from the pending-rating prompt (REP-01, UC-S-18)
- **Category:** ux-flow · **Severity:** Warning · **Certainty:** High
- **Location:** `apps/driver/src/components/ride/safety-actions.tsx:190-221`; `apps/driver/src/components/trust/rate-rider-screen.tsx:340-352`
- **Claims:** `trust:S-224`, `trust:U-189`
- **Rationale:** REP-01 (SRS.md:211) and UC-S-18 (shared.md:172-176) require reporting 'during or after a ride'. After-ride reporting exists only while a rating is pending. Partial, and no ruling restricts it.
  - UC-S-18 after-ride reporting for drivers is partial. This duplicates S-224.
- **Evidence:** Driver entry points to /report-rider are safety-actions.tsx:211 (in flight), reportHrefFor in rate-rider-screen.tsx:340-352 (used from home-screen.tsx:248, app/rate-rider.tsx:52 and apply-to-drive-screen.tsx:536, all PendingRating targets) and report-member-screen.tsx:85. The earnings trip rows (earnings-screen.tsx:282-300) link only to /rate-rider for pending ratings. app/reports.tsx is the 'my reports' list with no new-report entry. After the driver rates or skips, or the 7-day window ends, there is no path to report a past ride.
- **Fix Suggestion:** Add a 'Report rider' entry for past trips, for example on the earnings trip rows or a trip detail, including rides cancelled after acceptance. Design it on the canvas first.
