## What's good
- **The core loop is solid in the real apps.** Offer → accept → arrive (geofenced) → start → metered completion → cash confirmation → itemized receipt from stored components → tip and ledger worked first time across two apps and the backend. The money ledger stayed consistent through settlements, tips and commission debits.
- **Realtime is well built.** `offer.new` and `offer.withdrawn` arrive on time; chat over the shared socket is idempotent by `clientMsgId`; SOS reached the admin live map within a poll.
- **Suspension and floor states are clear in the UI.** The driver's "Ride offers paused" screen explains the debt and how to settle it, and an admin settlement unblocks the driver instantly.
- **i18n and RTL are thorough.** Both languages are complete across three clients, with Western digits, LTR-isolated money amounts in Arabic and a fully mirrored admin. The W1/W2 gaps are narrow.
- **The traceability discipline paid off.** Contract headers record rulings precisely enough that 15 claims could be ruled out by citation alone, and the verifiers could tell a deliberate ruling from an omission.
- **Design-first held.** 170 of 178 boards are implemented as approved or with minor differences.

## Items covered by approved deferrals or rulings
These are not gaps: an owner ruling, approved reading or accepted backlog item changes what "correct" means for them.

**Claims the verification wave resolved as covered (15):**

| Claim | Ruling / deferral that covers it |
|---|---|
| Backend has no pre-commit hook / install-hooks (`platform-nfr:S-307`) | The owner explicitly deferred this exact item (pre-commit hook and install-hooks) at the M1 closure, and the M1-T2 ticket itself scoped the Makefile without it. |
| Backend Makefile lacks install-hooks target (`platform-nfr:S-324`) | Same owner-agreed deferral as S-307, which covers the install-hooks target specifically. |
| Tests not colocated; admin uses Vitest (`platform-nfr:S-337`) | Jest-per-Nx-project with colocated *.spec.ts is an Angular/Nx shape. Vitest is the idiomatic runner for a Vite React app, and CLAUDE.md:18 explicitly forbids forcing template shapes. Testing is present and gated. |
| Admin files PascalCase without template suffixes (`platform-nfr:S-335`) | PascalCase component files are idiomatic React, and the template's suffix scheme is an Angular shape that CLAUDE.md:18 explicitly says not to force. |
| SOS admin push does not name the ride (`comms-safety:U-122`) | UC-A-20 step 1 'Alert names the ride' is met by the dashboard SOS alert. The push text is owner-approved copy. At most an Info-level nice-to-have. |
| waiting_on_user cannot move directly to resolved; admin must bounce via in_progress, pushing a misleading notice (`support:S-230`) | The M14-T1 contract documents this stricter state machine on purpose, and the owner signed off T1 (its AC was 'state machines documented'). The SRS chain describes the lifecycle, not every allowed edge. A user reply moves waiting_on_user to in_progress implicitly, so the normal path is waiting_on_user, then user reply… |
| Scripted e2e covers the backend only, not across the rider and driver apps (`rides-core:M-154`) | The owner accepted the backend simulator together with the live app runs as meeting the M7-T5 AC. The milestone passed review on that basis. |

## What was not verified, and why
**Live run:**
- **Real phones and iOS:** everything mobile ran on one Android emulator. iOS push is out of V1 scope by owner decision; real-device GPS, background location and battery behaviour were not exercised.
- **Real SMS and FCM:** both were deliberately disabled. The SMS gateway was replaced by a local receiver and push was log-only, so delivery, carrier filtering and push-tap routing were not exercised end to end.
- **Public OSM/OSRM:** used as configured; tiles stayed grey in headless Firefox. The ES-04 self-hosting migration is a launch-checklist item, not checked.
- **Other admin roles:** only the Super Admin logged in. Per-permission gating (Support role etc.) was verified by code reading and the existing `rbac-gating` tests, not by logging in as each role.
- **Flows not clicked through end to end:**
  - submitting a support ticket or a lost-item report from an app;
  - opening the public share page;
  - a driver application with document uploads, review and approval;
  - a vehicle switch and a document renewal;
  - drawing surge zones and peak windows in the admin;
  - CSV export downloads;
  - challenge and promo creation in the admin UI;
  - help-center CMS editing;
  - the invalid-promo error in the rider UI (blocked by the emulator's stylus tutorial).
- **Load and backups:** not re-run (M15 recorded its own load run and restore drill).
- **Full test suites:** not run, by owner rule (CI is the gate). Only the scoped tests below ran.

**Static-scan limits reported by the agents:**
- **analytics-admin:** Tests were not executed (read-only audit); test presence only. · Browser CSV download/save behaviour and the live-ops polling cadence at demo scale need a live run. · Owner approval of the M15 design boards (the canvas files exist, but approval is only a tracker claim). · Dashboard chart visual correctness against fixtures beyond the unit tests. · Shared-ride analytics edge cases: activeRiders counts only rides.rider_user_id (AnalyticsRepository.java:216), so non-creator members are never counted. grossFares uses fares.total before per-member promo discounts. Bot… · Live-ops driver click-through goes to /driver-ledgers (settlements:view), so an admin with only rides:view lands on Forbidden (Info).
- **app-admin:** Whether the owner-approved canvas boards show several primary buttons on Region detail and Help CMS. That decides whether the 5c deviation is real. · Leaflet controls and attribution rendering under dir=rtl (needs a live render). · Firebase background push click behaviour in a real browser. Inferred from the bundled @firebase/messaging SW source and the backend FcmPushSender payload; the gstatic compat build was assumed to be the same. · Whether whole-account suspension (user.state) also suspends the admin role assignment. The frontend mirrors the backend's role-state-only permission rule (RbacPermissionRepository.findCodesForActiveAdmin). · No tests were run (read-only audit rules).
- **app-driver:** Design-canvas parity of the settings diagnostics, the send icon and the support empty state: the canvas artifact was not opened. · On-device RTL after a direction reload, real safe-area rendering, and push delivery with cold-start tap routing need a live device run. · Only the notification-category family of the 78 dynamic t(`...${x}`) keys was checked. The others rely on i18n-keys.test.ts, whose coverage of dynamic keys was not fully read. · Backend behaviour for a rider-only account logging in with client=driver: the app-side gate is verified, the server response was not traced. · Whether the backend accepts an Accept-Language or device-language hint at registration (the driver client sends none).
- **app-rider:** Runtime RTL rendering after reload and Hermes Intl '-u-nu-latn' support on device (static review only) · Actual push-tap navigation on cold/warm start (logic read, not executed) · Whether dates/times should render in the region's time zone rather than device time zone (NFR-I2 'per region') · Unused exports across apps/rider/src (no knip configured) · Design-canvas owner approval of each screen (external artifact)
- **auth-accounts:** M-044/M-046: real SMS gateway end-to-end cycle needs a real handset and live run · No test suite was executed (backend integration/unit, rider/driver jest, admin vitest); only test presence checked · ArchUnit rules for M-036 not opened · Whether a deleted rider's scheduled rides / shared memberships / pending requests still dispatch (U-165) - ScheduledRideJob not traced · Rider translations.ts EN/AR parity only spot-checked for auth keys (driver/admin JSON parity verified: 0 missing) · M3-T4 manual scripted UI walkthrough and admin RTL rendering not observable statically · Behaviour of SMS_GATEWAY_TOKEN default 'change-me' in prod profile (application-prod.yml not inspected)
- **comms-safety:** Real-device rows M-123, M-127, M-162, M-224, M-227: only the code paths were checked. Actual FCM delivery on Android, iOS and web, and dialer behaviour, cannot be proven statically. · M-225 SOS path latency: the ordering (SOS first in the outbox query) and the post-commit dispatch pass are present in code, but latency was not measured. · None of the named tests (backend integration classes, app component tests) were run, because this audit is read-only. · Admin web push in a backgrounded tab depends on a deploy-time firebaseWeb block in config.json; not checked in a live browser. · Share-page Leaflet/OSM rendering under the strict CSP (a known backlog item, R-195) was not exercised. · Contract prose api/comms-v1.yaml:73 says the share-link response is {token, url}, while the schema (:229) and ShareLinkDto return {url, createdAt}. This is a documentation nit only, not raised as a gap.
- **dispatch-realtime:** M-124: GPS streaming on a real device and OSRM on the demo region are manual/device ACs and cannot be checked statically. Counted as implemented on code evidence. · S-129: whether the owner-approved design canvas leaves the route line off the rider active-ride map on purpose; the canvas was not inspected. · S-270 note: dispatch routes up to 5 candidates serially inside the ride-lock transaction (RideDispatchServiceImpl.java:161-170). With a blackholed OSRM (3 s timeout each), a dispatch step could take about 15 s and delay… · R-255: the admin web-push opt-in UI and the foreground toast/banner behaviour were only spot-checked (lazy Firebase import in admin/src/push/web-push.ts, 60 s unread poll). · No tests were run (read-only audit); every test class named is unexecuted evidence.
- **growth:** No backend or app tests were run, so whether the Growth* Testcontainers suites and the app growth tests pass is unknown. · EN/AR copy was checked by key parity only (rider growth-translations 123/123, driver 50/50, admin 148 keys, none missing); the wording was not proofread and the screens were not rendered. · Conformance to the approved canvas artboards (R13*/A13* boards) was not checked. · G4 depends on whether ruling 6's 'suspension' covers role-level suspension; the code clearly ignores role state. · G3 depends on whether a reactivated account with zero rides is meant to be excluded under the R-010 ruling that keeps /referrals/enter open. · Contract header drift: growth-v1.yaml:17-20 still says the driver gets a 'promo wallet credit' instead of driver-ledger promo_compensation, and stale code comments say referral defaults are 0 (GrowthRules.java, GrowthSe…
- **money:** No tests were run (read-only audit). Every test class named is unverified at runtime. · Rider and admin EN/AR parity was checked only by noting the existing parity tests (rider i18n/__tests__/translations.test.ts, admin test/i18n-parity.test.ts). The driver en.json/ar.json key sets were compared statically… · Visual/RTL/safe-area rendering of the money screens on a device or browser. · G2 rests on reading COM-03 'offers' to include scheduled-ride claims and conversion. No owner ruling was found either way. · The client formatMoney implementations (rider ride-format.ts, driver money/format.ts) were not opened, so NFR-I2 client display formatting was not checked line by line. · The scratch table is at /tmp/claude-1000/-home-azez--claude-spares-spare-ZqRtNI/a50873aa-c88c-4d5b-a67e-ed83f0306a4a/scratchpad/scan/money.md
- **onboarding:** No tests or builds were run (read-only audit). Test class names come from reading the source only. · Orphan cleanup race: when a FK violation happens at the deleteById commit, the code assumes a DataAccessException (caught) rather than a TransactionSystemException (uncaught, would abort the nightly pass). OrphanFileCle… · Whether a ride offer sent to a driver before an expiry suspension can still be accepted afterwards. The accept-path role check is in the rides slice. The candidate query (RideRepository.java:213-215) and forced-offline… · Info (row R-064, otherwise implemented): a driver whose role is suspended for document_expired cannot open the vehicles / vehicle-switch screens (apps/driver/src/app/_layout.tsx:61,87-94 require an active role). In the… · Info (S-275): the daily upload cap is check-then-insert without a lock (DriverFileServiceImpl store()), so parallel uploads can slightly exceed 50. The cap is shared with report/ticket uploads. · Rider DriverAvatar uses photoUrl without resolveFileUrl (apps/rider/src/screens/ride/ride-parts.tsx:154-156). That is the driver profile photo in the rides slice, not traced here. · M5-T5 live walkthrough (M-102) and design-canvas approval of the onboarding screens are process claims recorded in docs/milestones/README.md:103. They cannot be proven from code.
- **platform-nfr:** No builds, tests or servers were run (read-only rule): make check green, ArchUnit failing on a violation, /actuator/health UP, Swagger UI, emulator/simulator builds (M-005, M-006, M-008, M-009, M-014, M-016), CI run sta… · RTL visual correctness and EN/AR copy quality on every screen (S-281, M-330): only the automated key/parity/Arabic-Indic-digit tests and docs/qa/rtl-pass-2026-10-20.md were checked. · Load-test numbers and restore-drill timings were taken from the committed results docs and raw JSON, not reproduced (S-261..S-265, M-332..M-335). · NFR-U1 device floor: Expo SDK 57 default minSdk/iOS deployment target were not inspected; no explicit pin in app.config.ts. · Per-endpoint ownership checks (NFR-S3.a) and the 2FA/auth flows (ES-02) were only spot-checked; the feature slices own them. · Full table written to /tmp/claude-1000/-home-azez--claude-spares-spare-ZqRtNI/a50873aa-c88c-4d5b-a67e-ed83f0306a4a/scratchpad/scan/platform-nfr.md
